### Source Reviews

#### RateQuant: Optimal Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization via Rate-Distortion Theory

<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06675:start -->
- **Problem:** Large language models cache all previously computed key-value (KV) pairs during generation, and this KV cache grows linearly with sequence length, making it a primary memory bottleneck for serving.
- **Old path / changed constraint:** Large language models cache all previously computed key-value (KV) pairs during generation, and this KV cache grows linearly with sequence length, making it a primary memory bottleneck for serving.
- **Mechanism / ownership:** We present RateQuant , a framework that formalizes per-head KV cache bit allocation as rate-distortion optimization.
- **Evaluation contract:** We show, however, that such mixed-precision allocation has a hidden pitfall: each quantizer follows a different distortion curve D(b)=alpha*beta^{-b}, and the decay rate beta varies from 3.6 to 5.3 across quantizer designs.
- **Proof / non-proof:** Evidence is author-reported exact-v1 mechanism/evaluation evidence. It does not establish cross-model, cross-hardware, cross-workload or production generality unless those conditions are explicitly named above.
- **Trade-off / failure mode:** We call this failure mode distortion model mismatch and propose RateQuant to resolve it.
- **Coexistence boundary:** The older path remains appropriate where its workload and SLO do not trigger the changed constraint; the paper is treated as a conditional branch, not a universal replacement.
- **Primary:** [arXiv:2605.06675v1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06675v1)；frozen exact-v1 `papers/2026/05/_sources/arxiv-owner-replay-20260903/exact-v1/2605.06675v1.html.html`。
- **Disposition:** `No Change — Existing Coverage`；Fresh-context owner/adjacent comparison completed; the current Books proposition already owns the durable mechanism, so no duplicate paragraph was added.
<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06675:end -->
<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06675:end -->

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06676:start -->
#### LKV: End-to-End Learning of Head-wise Budgets and Token Selection for LLM KV Cache Eviction

<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06676:start -->
- **Problem:** Long-context inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) is bottlenecked by the linear growth of Key-Value (KV) cache memory.
- **Old path / changed constraint:** Long-context inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) is bottlenecked by the linear growth of Key-Value (KV) cache memory.
- **Mechanism / ownership:** To address this limitation, we introduce LKV (Learned KV Eviction), which formulates KV compression as an end-to-end differentiable optimization problem.
- **Evaluation contract:** Extensive evaluations demonstrate that LKV achieves state-of-the-art performance on both LongBench and RULER benchmarks at high compression rates.
- **Proof / non-proof:** Evidence is author-reported exact-v1 mechanism/evaluation evidence. It does not establish cross-model, cross-hardware, cross-workload or production generality unless those conditions are explicitly named above.
- **Trade-off / failure mode:** To address this limitation, we introduce LKV (Learned KV Eviction), which formulates KV compression as an end-to-end differentiable optimization problem.
- **Coexistence boundary:** The older path remains appropriate where its workload and SLO do not trigger the changed constraint; the paper is treated as a conditional branch, not a universal replacement.
- **Primary:** [arXiv:2605.06676v1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06676v1)；frozen exact-v1 `papers/2026/05/_sources/arxiv-owner-replay-20260903/exact-v1/2605.06676v1.html.html`。
- **Disposition:** `No Change — Existing Coverage`；Fresh-context owner/adjacent comparison completed; the current Books proposition already owns the durable mechanism, so no duplicate paragraph was added.
<!-- claim:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06676:end -->
<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06676:end -->

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07110:start -->
#### Securing Computer-Use Agents: A Unified Architecture-Lifecycle Framework for Deployment-Grounded Reliability

问题与演进：`Securing Computer-Use Agents: A Unified Architecture-Lifecycle Framework for Deployment-Grounded Reliability` 通过“Computer-use agents(CUAs)are moving frombounded benchmarks toward real software environments, wherethey operate browsers, desktops, mobile applications, flesystems,terminals, and tool backends.”改变 infer scheduling 的可观察机制或决策边界；exact-v1 的证明范围限于“The conclusion highlights open challengesin controllable grounding, long-horizon constraint preservation,safe authority binding, mixed-trust runtime defense, privacy-preserving memory,and continual assurance.”，不能外推到未披露 workload、model、hardware、precision、evaluator 或 SLO。旧方案在其原 workload、风险和成本约束下仍成立。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07110v1 §II Problem Formulation; §III Design Axes; §IV Tri-Layer Architecture — mechanism: Computer-use agents(CUAs)are moving frombounded benchmarks toward real software environments, wherethey operate browsers, desktops, mobile applications, flesystems,terminals, and tool backends. In such settings, reliability isno longer captured by task success alone: perception errors,planning drift, memory use, tool mediation, permission scope,and runtime oversight jointly determine whether agent actionsremain…`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07110v1 §VI Security Failure Modes; §VII Control Surface — disclosed scope: Computer-use agents(CUAs)are moving frombounded benchmarks toward real software environments, wherethey operate browsers, desktops, mobile applications, flesystems,terminals, and tool backends. <!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07111:start -->
### Beyond LoRA vs. Full Fine-Tuning: Gradient-Guided Optimizer Routing for LLM Adaptation

问题与演进：固定 LoRA 在低秩更新足够时节省状态，FFT 在高秩知识更新时保留容量；MoLF 让全部路径接收完整梯度，只由 optimizer 依据预条件下降分数稀疏提交更新。论文证据限于三任务、三个小模型和单卡实验，不能证明通用最优路由、训练吞吐或大模型稳定性；静态 LoRA/FFT 与显存受限的 MoLF-E 仍是共存分支。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07111v1 §4.1–§4.5 MoLF architecture、Sparse AdamW routing 与 fusion`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07111v1 §5.1–§5.4；Appendix A–B（3 tasks、Gemma/Qwen、single H100 or RTX PRO 6000、routing ablations）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07111v1 §6（first-order EPD、single FFT/LoRA expert、MoLF-E capacity boundary）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`exact-v1 未给出作者代码仓库；event-time commit Not Disclosed`。

### Region4Web: Rethinking Observation Space Granularity for Web Agents

问题与演进：Element-level AXTree 保留完整可操作对象但让页面功能结构和跨步变化隐式存在；Region4Web 先划分功能区域，PageDigest 再按任务选择区域并在同页增量维护 observation，选择不足时以 view_all 回退。收益与失败只在 WebArena 及披露 actor/evaluator 下成立，不能外推动态站点、隐藏 DOM 或生产浏览器安全。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07134v1 §3–§4（AXTree functional-region decomposition、semantic abstraction、PageDigest transition tracking）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07134v1 §5；Appendix C–G（WebArena 812 tasks、30-step cap、4 backbones、SteP/AgentOccam）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07134v1 Appendix A（AXTree/URL transition assumptions、selection and auxiliary-model errors）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

### Learning Agent Routing From Early Experience

问题与演进：始终调用 Agent 提高能力但承担高延迟，直接 LLM 更快却可能失败；BoundaryRouter 用部署早期经验构造边界样例，再以 rubric reasoning 决定升级。它把 routing proposal 与 Agent execution 分开，但早期经验质量、冷启动与分布漂移仍可能误路由；证据只覆盖 RouteBench 的二元路由，不能证明多 Agent 或高风险执行的授权正确性。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07180v1 §3（early-experience memory、rubric-guided chain-of-thought routing）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07180v1 §4–§5；Appendix A（RouteBench in-domain、paraphrase、OOD 与 ablations）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07180v1 §6（binary LLM-versus-one-agent routing；multi-agent/tool routing未验证）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`https://github.com/MiroMindAI/MiroFlow 为 exact-v1 链接的相关实现入口；paper-specific immutable commit Not Disclosed`。

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07514:start -->
### Is the Future Compatible? Diagnosing Dynamic Consistency in World Action Models

问题与演进：只评价 predicted video 或 action success 会漏掉二者是否描述同一转移；论文用执行后的真实 observation 定义 action-state consistency，并以候选 future consensus 构造无需环境重置的选择 proxy。该信号可能被静态背景主导且不能纠正 world model，真实环境仍拥有状态真值和动作提交权；结果只绑定两套 WAM、两套机器人 benchmark 与披露硬件。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07514v1 §2–§4（latent MSE consistency、environment-exploring upper bound、future-consensus selection）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07514v1 §5；Appendix C–G（Cosmos-Policy/RoboCasa、LingBot-VA/RoboTwin 2.0、N=8、8 RTX 5090）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07514v1 Appendix H（background collapse；selection不修复底层 dynamics）`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`exact-v1 仅链接 Cosmos-Policy 与 LingBot-VA 上游仓库；paper-specific implementation commit Not Disclosed`。

<!-- review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-07547:start -->
### Deadline-Driven Hierarchical Agentic Resource Sharing for AI Services and RAN Functions in AI-RAN

问题与演进：AI service 与 RAN 各自独占资源时责任清楚，却在 edge GPU 稀缺时浪费可共享容量；分层框架先提出 placement，再由 critic 估计 deadline 风险，allocation layer 才分配 GPU/CPU。收益来自论文负载与 SLO 定义，代价是预测误差、控制开销和高负载失稳；deadline 证据不足时仍应保留 reservation、静态优先级或拒绝 admission。

Method：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07547v1 §II–§III（placement candidate generator、predictive critic、deadline-aware GPU/CPU allocator）`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07547v1 §IV（ablation、overall SLO fulfillment、load sensitivity）`。

Non-proof / fallback：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.07547v1 §V；exact-v1 未建立跨 edge topology、不同 RAN/AI workload 或 production fault 的保证`。离开披露条件时回退既有机制，不把作者结果外推为通用保证。

Artifact：`exact-v1 未披露代码仓库或 immutable commit`。

<!-- review:SF-RECURSIVE-STATE-TERMINATION:start -->
### Recursive State and Termination

recursive reasoning 若只保存 transcript，就无法区分 claim、evidence、open question 与 confidence，也缺少可审计 stop signal。论文把它们显式为 epistemic state graph，并比较 expand→consolidate 与 consolidate→expand 的 order-gap；局部 gap 小表示两种更新近似交换。非退化定理只说明该 signal 何时不为代数空值，不证明 global convergence、正确性或成本最优，故应作为 termination sensor 与预算/外部 verifier 联用。

<!-- claim:SF-RECURSIVE-STATE-TERMINATION:start -->证据边界：只接受 arXiv exact-v1 在上述 method/evaluation contract 内的作者主张；未披露硬件、precision、并发、SLO、artifact commit 或生产条件写 Not Disclosed，不作外推。<!-- claim:SF-RECURSIVE-STATE-TERMINATION:end -->
<!-- review:SF-RECURSIVE-STATE-TERMINATION:end -->

<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06675:start -->对读 `books/part-05-inference-system/45-why-kv-cache-speeds-up.md#L97 (H2: KV Cache 的生命周期)` 及相邻章节后，现有命题为：本章的核心判断是：**KV Cache 利用 causal decoding 中历史 K/V 不再变化的性质，以随序列增长的 memory state 换取历史 layer computation 不重算；它加速 Decode，也把请求从无状态输入变成必须管理生命周期和 ownership 的系统对象。** 目标小节已经拥有该 family 所需的长期 owner 与旧路径/约束边界。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06675:end -->
<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06675:start -->Exact-v1 的 source-specific delta 是：We present RateQuant , a framework that formalizes per-head KV cache bit allocation as rate-distortion optimization. 其证据边界为：Evidence is author-reported exact-v1 mechanism/evaluation evidence. It does not establish cross-model, cross-hardware, cross-workload or production generality unless those conditions are explicitly named above. 该实现或实验没有改变当前章节已经成立的长期机制，不把作者 benchmark 外推为通用结论。<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2605-06675:end -->
Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`; reviewer=fresh-context:apr-may-books-20260903。
